Ericksonian
hypnosis - a 3 day intensive








in Melbourne










simply learning a natural approach to solutions in hypnosis





with Rob McNeilly









In
this 3 day intensive, CET offers a unique opportunity to learn the
principles and
applications of one of the most rapidly growing treatment models in
contemporary psychotherapy. The scope of the program is
comprehensive,
and presents the essential elements in an elegantly accessible way. 


The 3 day intensive is open to practitioners of all levels of
experience who are passionate about their work.






simply learning a natural approach to solutions in hypnosis 



simple


hypnosis
has been understood as complex, special, even dangerous, requiring
unique restrictions. In this programme we will explore simple, elegant
and respectful ways of connecting clients with their own unique styles
of experience.






learning


There will be a minimum of
theory and explanations, focusing instead on demonstrations and
recurrent practice so that participants will have sufficient skills to
begin to use their learning in their clinical practice immediately.






a natural approach


Erickson
spoke of the everyday trance as a natural experience that can be
enhanced in a clinical situation. By utilising individual
clients’
individual skills, a fail-safe method will be made obvious and readily
useable.






solutions


Ericksonian hypnosis creates a
wonderful context for a trusting therapeutic relationship, helping to
restore self-trust in individual clients by reconnecting them with
their natural resourcefulness.






hypnosis


When we
begin with the principle that hypnosis is an experience that involves
focus of attention and absorption in that experience, this natural
ability can be readily accessed by clients and incorporated in any
clinician’s practice.






3 day intensive


In this 3 day workshop, we will enjoy sharing learning:


creating a mood of connection to enhance rapport,


exploring natural links between everyday happenings 


and the process of
inviting a client into the experience of
hypnosis,


establishing easy and natural relationships between specific client
resources and their solutions.






Venue: 


Epilepsy Foundation of Victoria


181 Burke Road


Camberwell


  

Times:





9.00 - 5.00









Cost: $750 [incl GST]



Dates
:



Contact:


Rob McNeilly, 0433 273
352, rob@cet.net.au, www.cet.net.au

## 003-counselling-3-days

### counselling 3 days

*Mirrored page: `3dayc.html`* · *2 image(s): `rob.jpg`, `gabrielle.jpg`*

CET:
Centre of Effective Therapy

Rob McNeilly

Gabrielle Peacock



MBBS FRACGP







  
finding a therapist


  
The Milton H Erickson Istitute of
Tasmania


  


  
what's
new?


  
workshops for graduates


  
newsletter









 




















Diploma and Advanced
Diploma of 



Solution Oriented
Counselling






Overview


When Milton Erickson began working, inventing and experimenting with
therapy in the 1930’s there was little to choose from in
terms of
different therapy styles. In the1960's, there were about 60 different
therapy models, and today there are more than 250!!!! It is humbling to
note that despite the variety; virtually all of the research data finds
roughly equivalent results for all models. And perhaps we only need to
look at the world to see that that is so. In fact it is the
similarities rather than differences between the models that account
for the effectiveness of psychotherapy.





The Solution Oriented Approach honours these similarities, acknowledges
that many of the 250 have their roots in Erickson’s work and
in a
refreshing answer to today’s consumerism, attempts to
simplify
and unravel the confusion.





Michael Lambert’s 1992 research points to the existence of
four
factors common to all forms of therapy that contribute to change; 1:
extra therapeutic [40%]; 2: Client – therapist relationship
[30%]; 3: placebo, hope, and/or expectancy [15%]; and 4: structure,
model, and/or technique used[15%]. As humbling as this is we can see
that by creating a trusting, legitimizing relationship, observing and
listening to the client and reconnecting them to their life outside
therapy, utilising expectancy and trusting in our own individual
therapeutic creativity we can maximise our effectiveness. But how do we
observe and listen in order to most effectively provide a context for
the missing learning? 





This is the core of Erickson’s work specifically and the
Solution Oriented approach generally.





This diploma offers more than just information and techniques. It was
developed by Rob McNeilly and grounded in his 25 years of teaching and
learning including personally from Milton Erickson, Humberto Maturana,
Julio Ollala and Rafael Eccheveria to name a few. It is a rare
opportunity to have the ideas of such people distilled into one
approach with Rob’s uniquely creative, playful and
illuminating
style. Following 10 years working in General Practice with the inherent
frustrations of the medical model that appear common to doctors,
counsellors, psychologists and allied health workers alike, it was a
relief to discover a different way. As a student of Rob’s I
was
impressed by the refreshing learning experience he offered that
usefully allowed me to change how I observed and how I could use what I
saw to facilitate learning and change in a client. As a teacher of this
approach I have developed a passionate interest in the effectiveness of
this way of learning, in students and clients alike.





What you will learn


    •   
A solid foundation of Solution Oriented language forms.


    •   
Micro-observational skills and what to do with these observations.


    •   
Techniques for engaging even the most difficult clients.


    •   
Utilisation of the emotions and body in effecting change.


    •   
Familiarity and trust in your own creative process.


    •   
How to confidently adapt these ideas into your own style.





In the advanced diploma this learning will be extended by exploration of





  
listening


  
creating expectancy


  
generating trust


  
shifting unhelpful beliefs


  
self care












Course Details


The diploma involves 16 workshop days over 8 months and the advanced
diploma 10 workshop days over 10 month with lecture,
supported by written notes, discussion, but the emphasis is on actual
demonstrations and the opportunity for you to practice recurrently in
pairs or triads. This diploma values experiential learning over
acquisition of information such that you will feel confident to
incorporate these ideas into your work immediately.





Assignments, relevant to the actual clinical utilisation of the
learning offer valuable opportunities for reflection. Unlimited free
supervision and personal therapy is available throughout the course and
for 1 year following completion of the diploma. All diplomas can be
repeated free of charge at any time in the future.





The opportunity for supervised clinical experience will be available
in some locations for diploma graduates through a low cost community
clinic and provide valuable experience for future employment
opportunities.









Venue:


Epilepsy Foundation of Victoria


181 Burke Road


Camberwell


  



Times:


9.00 - 5.00






Dates






Registration



Cost 


Each diploma $3300 incl GST payable at the beginning of a programme, 


or 4 payments of $850 per term for each diploma

Ericksonian hypnosis - a 5 day intensive
















in Melbourne










October 8th - 12th, 2007































In
this 5 day intensive, CET with the Milton H Erickson Institute of
Victoria offers a unique opportunity to learn the principles and
applications of one of the most rapidly growing treatment models in
contemporary psychotherapy. The scope of the program is comprehensive,
and presents the essential elements in an elegantly accessible way. 







The 5 day intensive is open to practitioners of all levels of experience who are passionate about their work.
















simply learning a natural approach to solutions in hypnosis 








simple







hypnosis
has been understood as complex, special, even dangerous, requiring
unique restrictions. In this programme we will explore simple, elegant
and respectful ways of connecting clients with their own unique styles
of experience.
















learning







There will be a minimum of
theory and explanations, focusing instead on demonstrations and
recurrent practice so that participants will have sufficient skills to
begin to use their learning in their clinical practice immediately.
















a natural approach







Erickson
spoke of the everyday trance as a natural experience that can be
enhanced in a clinical situation. By utilising individual clients’
individual skills, a fail-safe method will be made obvious and readily
useable.
















solutions







Ericksonian hypnosis creates a
wonderful context for a trusting therapeutic relationship, helping to
restore self-trust in individual clients by reconnecting them with
their natural resourcefulness.
















hypnosis







When we
begin with the principle that hypnosis is an experience that involves
focus of attention and absorption in that experience, this natural
ability can be readily accessed by clients and incorporated in any
clinician’s practice.
















5 day intensive







In this 5 day workshop, we will enjoy sharing learning:







creating a mood of connection to enhance rapport,







exploring natural links between everyday happenings and the process of
inviting a client into the     experience of
hypnosis,







establishing easy and natural relationships between specific client resources and their solutions.
















Venue: 

"Cedar Lodge"

128 Murray Road

Diamond Creek



Melways Reference - Map 11, K1



















Cost: $1250 [incl GST]
























Contact:







Rob McNeilly, 0433 273 352, robmcneilly@cet.net.au, www.cet.net.au

Emma
Carmichael
 - Solution oriented
counselling, hypnotherapy including hypnobirthing and weight management
[Hobart] 





MBBS FRACGP








Emma Carmichael worked in general practice for ten years developing her
interests in family medicine, weight management and counselling. In
2005 Emma commenced learning with the Centre for Effective Therapy
Hobart, studying solution oriented counselling and hypnosis with Drs
Gabrielle Peacock and Rob McNeilly.





Emma now runs her own private counselling practice, and chooses to bulk
bill pensioners and health care card holders. Within this practice Emma
welcomes anybody wishing to discuss and work on any concerns, in a safe
and respectful environment. Emma is happy to see individuals, couples
and families.





As a GP, Emma developed an interest in weight management and eating
disorders. She uses a non dieting approach to weight management based
on the work of Dr Rick Kausman. His methods blend beautifully with
solution oriented couselling and hypnosis to offer individuals unique
opportunities to reach comfortable and sustainable weight and lifestyle
changes. The approach is also suitable for children.





Another area of special interest for Emma is hypnobirthing, (hypnosis
for pain relief in labour). With colleague Lisa Birrell, Emma offers an
evening course called
“Easy Labour”. The course is open to women, and
their
partners, seeking a more comfortable and empowered birthing experience.
The course runs over 6 weeks and is best suited to women in their last
trimester of pregnancy. Some private health funds provide a partial
rebate for these classes.





Phone 0411 042 609


Email everyday.solutions@hotmail.com 








Emma will be on maternity leave, late 2007 and early 2008.

THE MILTON H ERICKSON INSTITUTE OF TASMANIA






20th August, 2007














Milton H Erickson MD was one of the most
influential therapists of the 20th Century. He changed the direction of
therapy from classifying pathology to exploring resources – from
seeking out understandings of causes for problems to looking with each
individual for their own individual solutions. 













This solution approach is not just positive thinking. It is a 180
degree shift in direction. While some traditional methods are still
stuck in the old mold, many other schools of therapy have followed his
lead.













The Milton H Erickson Institute of Tasmania is delighted to provide
learning opportunities to health professionals who are interested to
support their clients to take charge of their lives again; to regain
their self-respect and autonomy; to get out from under the burden of
suffering and labelling which can only add to their difficulties.













This Institute is the 130th around the world to honour Erickson’s
massive contribution and to offer his approach to counsellors,
psychologists, social workers, doctors and other interested health
professionals.













The Ericksonian approach assumes that problems come when we become
disconnected from our strengths and abilities, and solutions appear
when the individual dignity of each client is respected, assuming their
resourcefulness, and supporting them to reconnect with their own unique
strengths and abilities. 













The Milton H Erickson Institute of Tasmania offers training programmes
in association with CET [The Centre of Effective Therapy] in 





   
 solution
oriented counselling, 



    solution oriented
family therapy and 



    solution oriented hypnosis. 





These programmes can
lead on the national accreditation through PACFA [Psychotherapy &
Counselling Federation of Australia], and participants can join a
supportive community for ongoing supervision and learning, as well as
assistance and support in moving into a career in private practice.










You are invited to explore details of our 
programmes
, our 
resources
, or 
contact us.











You are invited to join our learning community!



For information about The Milton H Erickson Foundation Inc ... 
click here

## 009-rob-mcneilly

### Rob McNeilly

*Mirrored page: `MHEIT/page1/page1.html`* · *1 image(s): `files/rob.jpg`*

The Milton H Erickson Institute of Tasmania

		

		

			
		

	

		
	

	

		

			
Robert McNeilly MBBS
Dr. Robert McNeilly was in a suburban Melbourne general medical practice for 10 years, had the privilege of learning directly with 
Milton Erickson
, was inspired by his human approach to therapy, and created his own interpretation to assist clients in a respectful, dignified way with the human dilemmas that affect individuals, couples and families. 
He founded the CET in l988 to introduce Ericksonian Hypnosis and the Solution Oriented Approach to hypnosis, counselling and coaching in Australia. For more than 30 years, the contribution he brings in his teaching style, writing and unique approaches to learning and therapy allow for a ready application into everyday living and are recognised locally, nationally and internationally, including repeated invited presentations at the Milton H Erickson Foundation’s International Congresses in USA since 1980 and workshops in Singapore and Denmark since 1999.
He is co-author, with Jenny Brown, of "Healing With Words", "Healing the Whole Person" was published by Wiley, republished as “Doing Change” by St Luke’s Innovative Resources, and Crown House have published a series of DVD demonstrations of counselling and hypnosis.
Students comment on his easy, respectful manner and his elegant ability to make complex issues approachable while retaining their essence. 
 
‘ Rob McNeilly studied with Milton H. Erickson M.D. ... is one of the most knowledgeable practitioners of Ericksonian methods in Australia. His teaching is clear, entertaining and edifying.’ 
Jeffrey K. Zeig, Ph.D. Board of Directors, The Milton H. Erickson Foundation, Inc., Phoenix, Arizona.
“Rob McNeilly worked with Milton Erickson, has been an invited presenter at International Ericksonian Congresses, and is a passionate and skilful teacher of the Solution Oriented Approach. I know him and his work, and value his personal and professional contributions."                    Bill O’Hanlon MS LMFT
‘A highly skilled presenter whose unique perspectives and playful demeanour make for a  delightful learning experience.’ 
Michael D. Yapko, Ph.D.
‘ .. Your clear and practical teaching of the solution oriented approach has enabled me to achieve more effective interventions with greater ease ..... the techniques are applicable in many different contexts...’ Peter Thorneycroft, Psychologist.
For more information, 
click here.

 or phone 0433 273 352

Solution
Oriented Family Therapy



a
3 day intensive





with Dr Gabrielle Peacock & Dr Robert McNeilly











In this 3 day workshop we will explore simple, elegant methods to
create a solution mood, and generate new ways for families to be
together. The focus will be on those elements beyond technique that are
common to all therapies that account for effectiveness, and we will
explore how we as therapists can actively generate these essential
ingredients of therapy.





This workshop will be of interest to anyone wanting to work with
children and their families, or to family therapists looking for a
forum to extend their work in a supported way. It provides an
opportunity for hands on learning around family therapy,
and even those working with families will benefit from this
experiential workshop which provides an opportunity to practice. 





This approach creates a context for a family to emerge from a group of
individuals while exploring the moves that make this possible. These
moves are foundational to this work. Managing the mood, joining, and
creating connections to unite a family group will be recurrently
emphasized, and this in turn will create the possibility to practice
with the family that emerges.





This interactive workshop emphasises demonstration and practice over
lecture presentation so that participants can expect to be able to use
the learning from this learning experience in their practice
immediately, and confidently.

Sarah De Jonge
 - Counselling for children, adolescents and adults.

BA, Grad DipSc - Psychology














"We can never really change someone; people change themselves. 


But we can help. We can be a resource." 


Stephen R. Covey
 





Usually people seek counselling because they have become stuck and are
uncertain about the best direction to follow. This can lead to boredom,
frustration, or despair. In these moments of uncertainty, I can help
you explore your situation, clarify the direction you wish to take and
discover your next step. I believe there is no set pathway in life, but
that each step we take creates a path that is uniquely our own. 



I began my studies with the Centre of Effective Therapy in 2005 and
have completed Diplomas in Solution Oriented Counselling and Family
Therapy. Along the way I gained three years practical experience
counselling and teaching with the Client-Centred model, and received a
Graduate Diploma of Science, specialising in Psychology. I am currently
continuing my education and training by furthering my studies in
Psychology at UTas and exploring hypnotherapy at CET.

. 

While my qualifications and experience provide a solid foundation for
my work, you will find my approach highly progressive and at times
unconventional. Ultimately, I am passionate about using my skills to
help you connect with what is most helpful for you as an individual.
It’s all about opening up new perspectives, creating
possibilities and generating momentum in your life.



I am always happy to consult with new clients of all ages, and will work with individuals, couples or families.

## 014-centre-of-effective-therapy

### Centre of Effective Therapy

*Mirrored page: `accreditation.html`* · *2 image(s): `Images/ASCHOAlogo.gif`, `Images/PACFAlogo.gif`*

Accreditation
 
Details

        All
        of Ampersand Australia’s diploma programmes have been carefully
        designed to achieve National Accreditation by complying with the minimal
        standards of ASOCHA [Association of Solution Oriented Counsellors and
        Hypnotherapists of Australia] which is a member organization of PACFA
      [Psychotherapy and Counselling Federation of Australia].

      
 After successfully
          completing a 2 year diploma or two diplomas with Ampersand Australia,
      Full Membership of ASOCHA becomes available.

      
 Ampersand
          Australia also provides further training and clinical supervision for
          anyone wanting to further their learning and achieve national recognition
          on the PAFCA National Register.

      

      

        

          
Click
              on the logos to go to their site.

Ben Nahmani































Bachelor of Social Work, Diploma in Social and Community Services, Member of the Australian Association of Social Workers















Ben offers a non-judgmental and supportive space for people to share
and explore difficulties and dilemmas, hopes and possibilities.  















Ben is both a therapist and social worker with extensive experience in
the human services sector.  Ben has a long-standing commitment to
working with individuals, couples and families in ways that acknowledge
and honour their unique worth, dignity, and potential. 















Ben also provides a consultancy and supervision service to agencies and
other professionals working within the human services sector.











Ben is an Approved Medicare Provider.











To make an appointment please call 0419 346 702 or 







Email:  bnahmani@gmail.com

finding a therapist



  
The Milton H Erickson Istitute of Tasmania



  



  
what's new?



  
workshops for graduates



  
newsletter















					











					

 











				











				




					











Ericksonian hypnosis - a 3 day intensive











in Brisbane













dates to be announced















































In this 3 day intensive, CET with the Milton H Erickson Institute of
Victoria offers a unique opportunity to learn the principles and
applications of one of the most rapidly growing treatment models in
contemporary psychotherapy. The scope of the program is
comprehensive, and presents the essential elements in an elegantly
accessible way. 










The 3 day intensive is open to practitioners of all levels of experience who are passionate about their work.






















simply learning a natural approach to solutions in hypnosis 











simple










hypnosis has been understood as complex, special, even dangerous,
requiring unique restrictions. In this programme we will explore
simple, elegant and respectful ways of connecting clients with their
own unique styles of experience.






















learning










There will be a minimum of theory and explanations, focusing instead on
demonstrations and recurrent practice so that participants will have
sufficient skills to begin to use their learning in their clinical
practice immediately.






















a natural approach










Erickson spoke of the everyday trance as a natural experience that can
be enhanced in a clinical situation. By utilising individual
clients’ individual skills, a fail-safe method will be made
obvious and readily useable.






















solutions










Ericksonian hypnosis creates a wonderful context for a trusting
therapeutic relationship, helping to restore self-trust in individual
clients by reconnecting them with their natural resourcefulness.






















hypnosis










When we begin with the principle that hypnosis is an experience that
involves focus of attention and absorption in that experience, this
natural ability can be readily accessed by clients and incorporated in
any clinician’s practice.






















3 day intensive










In this 3 day workshop, we will enjoy sharing learning:










creating a mood of connection to enhance rapport,










exploring natural links between everyday happenings and the process of
inviting a client into the     experience of
hypnosis,










establishing easy and natural relationships between specific client resources and their solutions.






















Venue:  Staff Club, University of Queensland - St Lucia Campus






















Cost: $750 [incl GST]











Registration






















Contact:










Rob McNeilly, 0433 273 352, rob@cet.net.au, www.cet.net.au

disclaimer

## 021-monthly-hypnosis-video-club

### monthly hypnosis video club

*Mirrored page: `club.html`* · *2 image(s): `rob.jpg`, `gabrielle.jpg`*

CET:
Centre of Effective Therapy

Gabrielle Peacock

finding a therapist

what's
new?





  
workshops for graduates





  
newsletter


















 






































monthly hypnosis video club











Serious students of hypnosis have been requesting opportunities to
observe hypnosis sessions with clients as well as participants in our
training programmes.











Since 2007, and with the creation of The Milton H Erickson Institute of
Tasmania, a number of clinicians have asked me to work with their
client to expand options, increase effectiveness, and
contribute
to our shared opportunities to learn together.











Each session is between 30 and 60 minutes duration, and are offered,
with sincere gratitude for the generosity
of the clients and their therapists, and confidentiality is required
and assumed.











Topics include self esteem, confidence, sexual abuse, weight issues,
marijuana use and pain, among a
varied and growing collection.











Members will be emailed a link, and notified as a new session is posted
each month. The previous month's session will be removed, although
these can also be purchased to have permanently for $A35.











Cost: $A55 per year [10 sessions].











If you have previous hypnosis training, are willing agree to
keep
this video confidential, only use it for your own learning experience,
and are interested to join this club, please fill out the form below
and post it with your payment to:











Rob McNeilly





CET





The Milton H Erickson Institute of Tasmania





336 Elizabeth Street





North Hobart TAS 7000

## 025-dip-soc

### Dip SOC

*Mirrored page: `counselling.html`* · *2 image(s): `rob.jpg`, `gabrielle.jpg`*

CET:
Centre of Effective Therapy
 



















  
About Us





  
Locations





  
MHEIT





  
Resources





  
News





  
Links





  
Contact Us





  
Blogs and Podcasts






















Rob McNeilly






MBBS






Gabrielle Peacock






MBBS FRACGP













  
finding a therapist





  
The Milton H Erickson Istitute of
Tasmania





  





  
what's
new?





  
workshops for graduates





  
newsletter


















 






































Diploma and Advanced
Diploma of 






Solution Oriented
Counselling












simply learning a
natural approach to solutions in counselling


















Background






This
diploma
offers more than just information and techniques. It was developed by
Rob McNeilly and grounded in 25 years of teaching and learning
including personally from Milton Erickson, Humberto Maturana, Julio
Ollala and Rafael Eccheveria to name a few. It is a rare opportunity to
have the ideas of such people distilled into one approach with
Rob’s uniquely creative, playful and illuminating style. It
was
extended by Gabrielle's 10 years experience in General Practice with
the inherent frustrations of the medical model that appear common to
doctors, counsellors, psychologists and allied health workers alike.
She was relieved to discover a different way. As a student of
Rob’s she was impressed by the refreshing learning experience
he
offered that usefully allowed her to change how she observed
and 
use what she saw to facilitate learning and change in a client. As a
teachers of this approach Rob & Gabrielle share a passionate
interest in the effectiveness of this way of learning for students and
clients also.












Overview






When Milton Erickson
began working,
inventing and experimenting with therapy in the 1930’s there
was
little to choose from in terms of different therapy styles. In the
1960's, there were about 60 different therapy models, and today there
are more than 250!!!! It is humbling to note that despite the variety,
virtually all of the research data finds roughly equivalent results for
all models. And perhaps we only need to look at the world to see that
that it is the similarities rather than differences between the models
that account for the effectiveness of psychotherapy.












The Solution Oriented
Approach
honours these similarities, acknowledges that many of the 250 have
their roots in Erickson’s work and in a refreshing answer to
today’s consumerism, attempts to simplify and unravel the
confusion.












Lambert's research in
1992 and
Wampole's in 2001 add statistical evidence supporting the core of
Erickson’s work specifically and the Solution Oriented
approach
generally - the importance of 






   
creating a trusting, legitimizing relationship, 






   
observing and listening to the client and reconnecting them to their
life outside therapy, 






   
creating expectancy and 






   
trusting in our own individual therapeutic creativity 






to maximise our
effectiveness. 












This diploma offers a
unique
opportunity to learn the principles and applications of one of the most
rapidly growing treatment models in contemporary psychotherapy. The
scope of the program is comprehensive, and presents the essential
elements in an elegantly accessible way and is open to practitioners of
all levels of experience who are passionate about their work.












simple






There is an explosion
of counselling models and approaches, each new model claiming to
be the best.  By exploring each individual's
experiences, we can have a simpler and more direct access for the
client to their own unique resources.












In this programme we
will explore simple, elegant and respectful ways of connecting clients
with their own unique resources.












learning






Traditionally learning
has been
based on theory and the property of a select few experts. By contrast,
these diplomas have a minimum of theory and explanations, focusing
instead on demonstrations and recurrent practice so that participants
will have sufficient skills to begin to use their learning in their
clinical practice immediately.












a natural approach






Counselling has been
assumed to be a
special, different, even weird experience. Erickson spoke of problems
being everyday experiences that get stuck, and so can also get unstuck
in a helpful way. By utilising individual clients’ individual
skills, overlooked skills can be disclosed, re-connected, and found to
be readily available, leading to a fail-safe method that will become
obvious to the client and so, readily useable.












solutions






Rather than problem
solving, the
solution approach draws on a client's innate resourcefulness,
re-connects them with these resources, and because they come from the
client, they can be easily accessible. Solution counselling creates a
wonderful context for a trusting therapeutic relationship, helping to
restore self-trust in individual clients to allow this process to be
realised.












counselling






Traditional counselling
relies on
theories and manualised protocols which are applied to clients in a
"fit where it touches" approach, creating resistance and limited
compliance. When we begin with the principle that any problem has a
solution waiting to emerge, this natural progression can be readily
accessed by all clients and incorporated in any clinician’s
practice.












What you will learn











  
The
evolution of counselling throughout recent history.
    





  
A
method of creating genuine rapport.





  
Questions
to connect clients with their overlooked resources.





  
Conversations
to engage clients in their counselling process.





  
How to
create possibilities even in the most "resistant" or "chronic"
situations.





  
How to
include moods and emotions in solutions





  
How to
design strategies





  
Positive
reframing





  
How to
apply this approach to:





  
  
  
  





    
anxiety,
panic & depression





    
post
trauma





    
childhood
abuse





    
pain
management





    
couples
work





    
family
relations





    
performance
enhancement





    
coaching





    
any
other clinical situation





  
  
  
  


















In
the advanced diploma this learning will be extended by











  
   
reviewing the diploma experiences





  
   
practicing advanced counselling approaches





  
   
listening for solutions





  
   
creating expectancy





  
   
generating genuine trust





  
   
shifting unhelpful beliefs





  
   
individualising self care


















Course Details






The
diploma involves 14 workshop days over 8 months and the advanced
diploma 10 workshop days over 10 month with lecture, supported by
written notes and discussion. Assignments, relevant to the actual
clinical utilisation of the learning offer valuable opportunities for
reflection and any diploma can be repeated free of charge at any time
in the future. The emphasis is on live demonstrations and the
opportunity for you to practice recurrently in pairs or triads. These
diplomas value experiential learning over acquisition of information so
that you will feel confident to incorporate these ideas into your work
immediately.












Summary






These
diplomas provide learning opportunities which are individualised,
practical, relevant, easily understood, readily translatable into
clinical practice, all in a safe environment of respectful support.















Hobart Venue:






CET,
The Milton H Erickson Institute of Tasmania






The
Coach House






336
Elizabeth Street






North
Hobart TAS 7000






Times:
9.00 - 3.00










Cost 





Each diploma $3300 incl GST payable at the beginning of a programme, 





or 8 monthly payments of $425 beginning April, 2009.



























Melbourne Venue:


Blue Angel Gallery


67 Glenferrie Road


Malvern 3144





Times: 9.00 - 5.00








Cost 





The diploma is $4400 incl GST payable at the beginning of a programme, 





or 8 monthly payments of $550 beginning April, 2009.


The advanced diploma is $3300 or 8 monthly payments of $425 beginning February, 2009.

## 026-curiologists

### curiologists

*Mirrored page: `curiologists.html`* · *3 image(s): `rob.jpg`, `gabrielle.jpg`, `longer%20logo.jpg`*

CET:
Centre of Effective Therapy
 










  
About Us


  
Locations


  
MHEIT

Contact Us


  
Blogs and Podcasts













Rob McNeilly



MBBS



Gabrielle Peacock



MBBS FRACGP







  
finding a therapist


  
The Milton H Erickson Istitute of
Tasmania


  


  
what's
new?


  
workshops for graduates


  
newsletter









 




























The Milton H Erickson Institute
of Tasmania





 in
conjunction with






The Centre of Effective Therapy






presents









Fridays 12:30 - 1:30 pm









Free
lunchtime meetings for curiologists:





conversations with giants











presented by Rob McNeilly and Gabrielle Peacock









March 28 -
Introduction to Milton H Erickson 





May 2 - Introduction to Humberto Maturana





June 20 - Introduction to Heinz von Foerster





August 8 - Maturana’s biology of cognition





October 10 - Introduction to Werner Erhard, Bob Hoffman & Harry
Palmer





Heinz on ethics





Introduction to G Spencer Brown





Maturana’s responsibility and freedom





Ericksonian Hypnosis





Heinz on trivial and non trivial machines





Neil Postman’s education as a subversive act





The Partners for Change (Miller, Duncan, Wampold)





Introduction to Ontological Coaching






Venue:
The coach house 336 Elizabeth Street North Hobart Ph 0433 273 352 b.y.o
lunch





Through this series we would like to introduce curious learners to the
ideas of some of the most influential minds of our time. We have chosen
to begin this series by introducing you to our most valued teachers and
hope to extend this to contributions from members of our growing
learning community.







Milton H Erickson MD
 1901-1980 was one of the most
influential therapists of the 20th 


Century. He changed the direction of therapy from classifying pathology
to exploring 


resources – from seeking out understandings of causes for
problems to looking with each individual for their own individual
solutions. He was an explorer in therapy, still learning, exploring and
teaching right up till his death. His own personal struggles with polio
and post polio syndrome provided real life experience and personal
exploration with the hypnotic experience. he believed that people
didn’t come to therapy to change an unchangeable past, but
with
some dissatisfaction with the present and a wish for a better future.
Rob spent time with Erickson towards the end of his life and brings his
personal connection to this introduction of the man behind the purple
suit.






Humberto Maturana
 is
a Chillean
neurobiologist who has radically transformed our understanding of the
processes of life and cognition. He has become famous as a leading
exponent of modern systems thinking and is the author of several books,
including the bestseller written with Francisco J Varella, The Tree of
Knowledge. Some of his more interesting reflections include his time in
Chile under the Pinochet dictatorship. He was interested to understand
the dictatorship, and how, as in Nazi Germany, how people gradually go
blind, and what the causes of such perceptual deprivation were. the
experience provided a real life opportunity to observe observing.
Maturana came to Melbourne several times in the 90’s and Rob
attended his lectures. We would like to introduce you to our take on
the man, and share something of what he is about. Later in the series
we will look in more depth at some of his important contributions.






Heinz von Foerster

1911-2002 has
been variously dubbed a physicist, mathematician, cybernetician, but he
preferred to call himself a ‘curiologist’. He would
also
admit to being Viennese! A colleague and friend of Humberto Maturana,
Von Foerster is credited with ceaselessly pointing out the unavoidable
biases and blind spots on the part of the observers, who approach the
object of description that they perceive to be independent of
themselves. He trained as a physicist in Vienna, worked as a magician
throughout the war years and wrote his first book Memory: a quantum
physical study, which attracted the pioneers of American cybernetics
and led to an invitation for him to join them in 1949. He joined a rich
and fertile group of scientists who came together in the 50’s
at
the invitation of the Macy Foundation, names such as John Von Neumann,
the inventor of the computer, anthropologists Margaret Mead, Gregory
Bateson, as well as neuropsychiatrist Warren McCulloch and others. His
clarity and whimsy bring a rare mood to looking at otherwise difficult
ideas. We never met him, the closest we get is a video of conversations
between him and Maturana on ‘truth and
trust’  yet we
have developed a great fondness and gratitude to the man and his
contributions. As with Maturana we would like to introduce you to the
man, and later in the series we will look at some of his important
contributions 






More to follow in 2008






Registration

MBBS





Gabrielle Peacock





MBBS FRACGP











  
finding a therapist




  
The Milton H Erickson Istitute of
Tasmania




  




  
what's
new?




  
workshops for graduates




  
newsletter















 












Distance programmes




with Dr. Robert McNeilly MBBS & Dr Gabrielle Peacock MBBS FRACGP










Principles




The learning experience of these programmes is both unique and
everyday.
The uniqueness is in its almost total lack of theory and formal
structure; the everydayness is in the way the learning is personal and
relates to any individual and their everyday experiences.









For millennia learning has been the prerogative of the few, usually
wealthy or privileged. Only over the last half century has this begun
to change. Academic circles can still maintain a sense of elitism,
and while the approach of this programme does not have any argument
with academia, it has a radically different focus.









Because these programmes give precedence to experience over theory,
curiosity over fixed explanations, and individual differences over
categorising into groups or classes, participants can expect to be more
like themselves and less like any of those who may already be seen as
expert in the field. Previous participants comment that their
individuality is enhanced – personal and professional
– and
their individual style of working becomes more obvious as the programme
unfolds.










Structure




All programmes include recorded lectures, an e-book, videos of demonstrations,
written assignments, supervision, and most important of all, practice.
Ultimately it is what participants learn and take into their clinical
experience that will be crucial. Anything that is heard, read, observed
or discussed, including personal growth, will become relevant as it
incorporated in life or work.









At the beginning of each programme, notes and written assignments are
distributed by email, also relevant links to lectures and
demonstrations. Participants can then progress
though their learning experience at their own pace.









All participants are encouraged to form study groups with others as an
important component of the learning process, many people
reporting that they are the most relevant. Individual supervision is
another important component, whether face to face, email, phone, peer
supervision or some combination of these alternatives. Written
assignments add to the breadth of the learning and there is often an
expression of surprise when they are discovered to be enjoyable as well
as useful.









Most people report that 2 to 3 hours a week is more than enough to
cover the individual work outside any workshops and study group
experiences.









Because of the solid foundations of the approach, there are many
options for individual learning styles and outcomes, and participants
can expect to develop confidence in their own individual style of
learning and working.










Accreditation




These programmes are accredited by CET, as
evidence shows overwhelmingly that external accreditation has no
correlation with effectiveness of learning or application.










Face to face Workshops

There will be local face to face workshops organised depending on
numbers and participants are welcome to join in at any other location
during the programme and any time in the future without limitation.










Fees




$4400 or 4 term payments of $1100 [including GST]










Diploma of Solution Oriented Hypnosis  










Content




The diploma is structured into 4 terms.




In the first term we explore hypnotic language, hypnotic invitations,
and structuring a hypnotic session.




In the second term we include the experience of hypnosis including the
classical hypnotic phenomena.




In the third and fourth term we focus on the clinical application of
this approach as well as supervision issues.





























Diploma of Solution Oriented Counselling










Content




The diploma is structured into 4 terms.




In the first term we explore the effective use of language in
counselling;




In the second term we include emotions and the body as an integral part
of counselling as well as strategies and positive reframing;




In the third and fourth term we focus on the clinical application of
this approach as well as supervision issues.

























Diploma of Solution Oriented Family Therapy










Content




The diploma is structured into 4 terms.




In the first term we explore the effective use of questions to
reconnect family members;




In the second term we include emotions and the body as an integral part
of family therapy as well as strategies and positive reframing;




In the third and fourth term we focus on the clinical application of
this approach as well as supervision issues.

Ericksonian
hypnosis - a 3 day intensive












in Hobart
















simply learning a natural approach to solutions in hypnosis




















In this 3 day intensive, CET with the Milton H Erickson Institute of
Tasmania offers a unique opportunity to learn the principles and
applications of one of the most rapidly growing treatment models in
contemporary psychotherapy. The scope of the program is comprehensive,
and presents the essential elements in an elegantly accessible way. 




The 3 day intensive is open to practitioners of all levels of
experience who are passionate about their work.

simple




hypnosis has been understood as complex, special, even dangerous,
requiring unique restrictions. In this programme we will explore
simple, elegant and respectful ways of connecting clients with their
own unique styles of experience.










learning




There will be a minimum of theory and explanations, focusing instead on
demonstrations and recurrent practice so that participants will have
sufficient skills to begin to use their learning in their clinical
practice immediately.










a natural approach




Erickson spoke of the everyday trance as a natural experience that can
be enhanced in a clinical situation. By utilising individual
clients’ individual skills, a fail-safe method will be made
obvious and readily useable.










solutions




Ericksonian hypnosis creates a wonderful context for a trusting
therapeutic relationship, helping to restore self-trust in individual
clients by reconnecting them with their natural resourcefulness.










hypnosis




When we begin with the principle that hypnosis is an experience that
involves focus of attention and absorption in that experience, this
natural ability can be readily accessed by clients and incorporated in
any clinician’s practice.










3 day
intensive




In this 3 day workshop, we will enjoy sharing learning:




creating a mood of connection to enhance rapport,




exploring natural links between everyday happenings and the process of
inviting a client into the    
experience of
hypnosis,




establishing easy and natural relationships between specific client
resources and their solutions.










Venue:






The Coach House




336 Elizabeth Street




North Hobart 7000










Cost:

$495 [incl GST] - 50%student discount





Registration





Dates:

:

www.cet.net.au

## 031-counselling

### counselling

*Mirrored page: `hobartc.html`* · *2 image(s): `rob.jpg`, `gabrielle.jpg`*

CET: Centre of Effective Therapy

Blogs and Podcasts















				
















			















			


				


					

Rob McNeilly















MBBS















					

Gabrielle Peacock















MBBS FRACGP















					


						


  
finding a therapist

  
The Milton H Erickson Istitute of Tasmania

  

  
what's new?

  
workshops for graduates

  
newsletter

















					















					

 















				















				



























































Diploma and Advanced Diploma of 









Solution Oriented Counselling


















Overview








When Milton Erickson began working, inventing and experimenting with
therapy in the 1930’s there was little to choose from in terms of
different therapy styles. In the1960's, there were about 60 different
therapy models, and today there are more than 250!!!! It is humbling to
note that despite the variety; virtually all of the research data finds
roughly equivalent results for all models. And perhaps we only need to
look at the world to see that that is so. In fact it is the
similarities rather than differences between the models that account
for the effectiveness of psychotherapy.

















The Solution Oriented Approach honours these similarities, acknowledges
that many of the 250 have their roots in Erickson’s work and in a
refreshing answer to today’s consumerism, attempts to simplify
and unravel the confusion.

















Michael Lambert’s 1992 research points to the existence of four
factors common to all forms of therapy that contribute to change; 1:
extra therapeutic [40%]; 2: Client – therapist relationship
[30%]; 3: placebo, hope, and/or expectancy [15%]; and 4: structure,
model, and/or technique used[15%]. As humbling as this is we can see
that by creating a trusting, legitimizing relationship, observing and
listening to the client and reconnecting them to their life outside
therapy, utilising expectancy and trusting in our own individual
therapeutic creativity we can maximise our effectiveness. But how do we
observe and listen in order to most effectively provide a context for
the missing learning? 

















This is the core of Erickson’s work specifically and the Solution Oriented approach generally.

















This diploma offers more than just information and techniques. It was
developed by Rob McNeilly and grounded in his 25 years of teaching and
learning including personally from Milton Erickson, Humberto Maturana,
Julio Ollala and Rafael Eccheveria to name a few. It is a rare
opportunity to have the ideas of such people distilled into one
approach with Rob’s uniquely creative, playful and illuminating
style. Following 10 years working in General Practice with the inherent
frustrations of the medical model that appear common to doctors,
counsellors, psychologists and allied health workers alike, it was a
relief to discover a different way. As a student of Rob’s I was
impressed by the refreshing learning experience he offered that
usefully allowed me to change how I observed and how I could use what I
saw to facilitate learning and change in a client. As a teacher of this
approach I have developed a passionate interest in the effectiveness of
this way of learning, in students and clients alike.

















What you will learn








    •    A solid foundation of Solution Oriented language forms.








    •    Micro-observational skills and what to do with these observations.








    •    Techniques for engaging even the most difficult clients.








    •    Utilisation of the emotions and body in effecting change.








    •    Familiarity and trust in your own creative process.








    •    How to confidently adapt these ideas into your own style.





In the advanced diploma this learning will be extended by exploration of





  
listening


  
creating expectancy


  
generating trust


  
shifting unhelpful beliefs


  
self care






























Course Details








The diploma involves 16 workshop days over 8 months and the advanced diploma 10 workshop days over 10 month with lecture,
supported by written notes, discussion, but the emphasis is on actual
demonstrations and the opportunity for you to practice recurrently in
pairs or triads. This diploma values experiential learning over
acquisition of information such that you will feel confident to
incorporate these ideas into your work immediately.





Assignments, relevant to the actual clinical utilisation of the
learning offer valuable opportunities for reflection. Unlimited free
supervision and personal therapy is available throughout the course and
for 1 year following completion of the diploma. All diplomas can be
repeated free of charge at any time in the future.






The opportunity for supervised clinical experience will be available
in some locations for diploma graduates through a low cost community
clinic and provide valuable experience for future employment
opportunities.



























Dates















Registration






Cost 


Each diploma $3300 incl GST payable at the beginning of a programme, 








or 4 payments of $850 per term for each diploma

Diploma of Solution Oriented Family Therapy



Overview


This diploma will be of interest to anyone wanting to work with
children and their families, or to family therapists looking for a
forum to extend their work in a supported way. This programme provides
an opportunity for hands on, experiential learning around family
therapy, and even those working with families will benefit from these
experiential workshops which provide an opportunity to practice. This
programme creates a context for a family to emerge from a group of
individuals while exploring the moves that make this possible. These
moves are foundational to this work. Managing the mood, joining, and
creating connections to unite a family group will be recurrently
emphasized, and this in turn will create the possibility to practice
with the family that emerges.





CET diplomas have been based in Melbourne and running nationally for 25
years, providing a solid foundation grown from this experience and the
feedback of hundreds of students about what is useful in a learning
environment. These diplomas provide a refreshing return to emphasising
a client’s actual experience, and away from the heady
intellectualisations that have been distilled as techniques. This work
has evolved from its origins in Rob McNeilly’s time spent with
Milton Erickson in the 70’s, through his working and teaching to
the present day. In our teaching we emphasise demonstration and
practice over lecture and information. Where Erickson was a consummate
teacher, we are interested in the experience of learning. Rob has
explored this face to face with teachers such as Rafael Eccheverria,
Humberto Maturana, Julio Olalla and Fernando Flores to name a few, and
has brought this learning to teaching ,creating a community of
educators locally, nationally and internationally





Lecture and content in these programmes takes on a very different feel
for the student as we ‘speak it’, rather than ‘speak
about it’. A meticulous focus on language which is generative
shows a consistency of approach in our working, teaching and
supervision of students. The generative importance of emotions and the
body is similarly coherently expressed.





We invite students to observe a demonstration session, a client, a
lecture, a book or paper and even their own writing from within their
own experience, and to explore their own individual response and
learning. Students learn a way of individualising the therapy to the
client, while being in the experience of having the teaching
individualised to them in a way that allows a texture of experience
that is rare in modern education practices.





In this approach diagnoses are acknowledged and then the individual is
allowed to emerge in his/her entirety, uncovering concerns, strengths,
resources and often banal human dilemmas. At face value this way of
working reveals engaging, practical and deceptively simple sessions,
which provide an experiential blueprint from which to practice,
allowing exploration of the many layers and subtleties of the use of
language, emotions and the body to generate learning which is
sustainable and available for future experience as well as past
reflection.





In my own learning, initially in these programmes beginning in 2000 and
then as a kind of Sorcerers Apprentice to Rob, learning to teach and
teaching to learn, I have developed a deep appreciation of the value of
experiential learning, and a fascination in facilitating learning in
others. My interest in Family therapy has grown from 11 years in a
family based general practice, with the added joy for me in learning to
be part of a solution oriented conversation that reconnects a family,
articulates concerns and uncovers family strengths and resources that
assists their being together in a more satisfying and sustaining way.






An Invitation


My purpose here is to invite anyone already working with individuals or
couples to play with transferring their talents to working with
families, and to provide a forum for exploration and practice for any
family therapist to extend themselves in a safe and supported way.


What you will learn


    •    A map to simplify the complexity of a family’s presenting issues.


    •    Competence and confidence in managing the mood within a family and group.


    •    Leading with that mood and the possibilities for the actions that can emerge


    •    A way of observing that
will allow increased sensitivities to maintaining this mood throughout
a difficult conversation


    •    The use of language as a generative tool for creating solutions


    •    Ways of working with
emotions and the body to add texture to the solution experience.


    •    A framework for running a
family session   where each participant’s individual
style of therapy can be incorporated and built on.


    •    Clinical applications dealing with commonly encountered family problems.


 



Dates






Cost 


$3300 incl GST payable at the beginning of a programme, or 4 payments of $840 per term

Solution Oriented Family Therapy


A one day introduction


In this 1 day workshop we will explore simple elegant methods to create
a solution mood, and generate new ways for families to be together. The
focus will be on those elements beyond technique that are common to all
therapies that account for effectiveness, and we will explore how we as
therapists can actively generate these essential ingredients of therapy.





This interactive workshop emphasises demonstration and practice over
lecture presentation so that participants can expect to be able to use
the learning from this learning experience in their practice
immediately, and confidently.  








Presenter:   Rob McNeilly and Gabrielle Peacock 


dates - TBA


Tuition $165 [including 10% GST]












Learning Solutions in Hypnosis


An Introduction to the Ericksonian Approach


Topics will include:-


    •  
 the nature and experience of hypnosis


    •  
 the language of hypnosis


    •  
 therapeutic metaphor and


    •  
 the induction and utilisation of the hypnotic experience.





Presenter:   Rob McNeilly






Learning Solutions in Counselling


An Introduction to the Solution Orientation





Participants will be offered:-


    •  
 explanations of theoretical
and practical issues related to the latest developments in the field;


    •  
 maps to more effectively
negotiate the process of engaging the client in their therapeutic
process;


    •  
 demonstrations and
supervised practice to gain confidence generating effective solutions
with clients, whether face to face or in telephone conversations.





Presenter:   Rob McNeilly

After
initial qualifications in accounting disciplines, Justin worked in a
variety of sales and commercial roles with a number of multi national
companies. Justin now focuses on coaching and training teams and
individuals within organisations using a solutions orientation. 


 


This includes: executive coaching, executive skills based coaching, bid
management, leadership development, presentation skills programs,
negotiation skills programs, selling skills programs, team development
and facilitation.


 


Justin has an Advanced Diploma in Solutions Orientated Coaching, and a
Bachelor of Commerce from the University of Tasmania. He is also
qualified to administer the Strength Deployment Inventory®, and has
is also certified to administer the Mayer-Salovey-Caruso Emotional
Intelligence Test (MSCEIT®).





Based in Melbourne, Justin is proud of his native Tasmania, where he
regularly consults and trains when he’s not at home with his wife
and three children.

## 038-lasting-solutions

### lasting solutions

*Mirrored page: `lasting_solutions.html`* · *2 image(s): `rob.jpg`, `gabrielle.jpg`*

CET:
Centre of Effective Therapy

what's
new?


  
workshops for graduates


  
newsletter









 














creating
lasting solutions in counselling



connecting clients with
their experiences








We want solutions for our troubled client, and we want the solutions to
last.





The solution approach to counselling is recognised as one of the most
effective methods of assisting clients to resolve their problems and
create their own individual solutions. It builds on the ground-breaking
contributions of Milton Erickson, Steve de Shazer, Insoo Kim Berg, and
Bill O'Hanlon.





Even after a client finds a solution that is effective, concerns remain
- will they act on it; will it happen, will it last?





Problems often capture our attention and take over clients' experience,
blinding them to their solutions, or even the possibility of a solution.





In this workshop we will explore simple and effective ways of
connecting clients with the experience of their solutions,
moving
them past the idea, the hope, the wish of a resolution.





We will learn through lecture, demonstration and supervised practice
the steps that will assist clients 


    to focus on useful experiences - past,
present and future


    to make richly textured connections with
these experiences


    to assist the client to absorb and own
the experiences of their solutions


    to learn how to shift their focus from
problem to solution


so they can own their solutions and take them into their own lives
outside the counselling setting.





Because the workshop will be practical and experiential, participants
will be able to translate their learning into their clinical practice
in a natural and effective manner.





Dates:
 





Times: 9.00 am - 3.00 pm





Venue:    


CET The Coach House


336 Elizabeth Street


North Hobart 7000





Cost:    $125 






If
you're interested, you're invited!





Rob McNeilly & Gabrielle Peacock






Registration

Liz McArthur






B.A. (Psychology), Post. Grad. Dip (Psychology), Advanced Dip. Learning
Solutions in Hypnotherapy, Cert. IV Health (Clinical Hypnosis) Cert. IV
(Telephone Counselling)


 


I have a background in psychology and counselling.  After training
in and practicing more conventional hypnotherapy I decided to explore
solution focused hypnotherapy.  I love the way this approach
rapidly builds clients’ connection with and confidence in their
own strengths and their ability to resolve concerns.


 


I believe in clients' inner healing capacity and see my role as
respectfully facilitating that process, efficiently assisting them to
discover their own unique path to the future they desire.


 


I’m interested in working with all kinds of concerns, from
becoming more relaxed and comfortable in certain situations, creating
healthy habits, enhancing relationships and unlocking inner creativity
to improving personal performance for example, sporting performance.


 


I practice in Elwood.  9531 7265, 0407 838 505 or lizmca@cet.net.au

## 044-melbourne-locations

### Melbourne | Locations

*Mirrored page: `locations/page21/page21.html`* · *5 image(s): `files/yes-3.jpg`, `files/frontpagepic150.png`, `files/bill-chin-on-books_2.jpg`, `files/mychael-yapko.jpg`, `files/rob-0026-peter_3-2.jpg`*

Locations

		

	

	
	

		
	

	
	

		

			
		
 
		
		

			

			
Rob McNeilly MBBS 
was in a suburban Melbourne general medical practice for 10 years, had the privilege of learning directly with 
Milton Erickson
, was inspired by his human approach to therapy, and created his own interpretation to assist clients in a respectful, dignified way with the human dilemmas that affect individuals, couples and families.

Rob McNeilly MBBS 
was in a suburban Melbourne general medical practice for 10 years, had the privilege of learning directly with 
Milton Erickson
, was inspired by his human approach to therapy, and created his own interpretation to assist clients in a respectful, dignified way with the human dilemmas that affect individuals, couples and families. 
He founded the CET in l988 to introduce Ericksonian Hypnosis and the Solution Oriented Approach to hypnosis, counselling and coaching in Australia. For more than 30 years, the contribution he brings i his teaching style, writing and unique approaches to learning and therapy allow for a ready application into everyday living and are recognised locally, nationally and internationally, including repeated invited presentations at the Milton H Erickson Foundation’s International Congresses in USA since 1980 and workshops in Singapore and Denmark since 1999.
He is co-author, with Jenny Brown, of "Healing With Words", "Healing the Whole Person" was published by Wiley, republished as “Doing Change” by St Luke’s Innovative Resources, and Crown House have published a series of DVD demonstrations of counselling and hypnosis.
Students comment on his easy, respectful manner and his elegant ability to make complex issues approachable while retaining their essence. 
‘ Rob McNeilly studied with Milton H. Erickson M.D. ... is one of the most knowledgeable practitioners of Ericksonian methods in Australia. His teaching is clear, entertaining and edifying.’ 
Jeffrey K. Zeig, Ph.D. Board of Directors, The Milton H. Erickson Foundation, Inc., Phoenix, Arizona.
“Rob McNeilly worked with Milton Erickson, has been an invited presenter at International Ericksonian Congresses, and is a passionate and skilful teacher of the Solution Oriented Approach. I know him and his work, and value his personal and professional contributions."                    
Bill O’Hanlon MS LMFT
A highly skilled presenter whose unique perspectives and playful demeanour make for a  delightful learning experience.’ 
Michael D. Yapko, Ph.D.
‘ .. Your clear and practical teaching of the solution oriented approach has enabled me to achieve more effective interventions with greater ease ..... the approach is applicable in many different contexts...’ 
Peter Thorneycroft, Psychologist.
  

			 

		

	

	
	

		

			
Ericksonian Hypnosis - a 3 day intensive
Milton Erickson's flexibility was legendary, and many experts have tried to understand and codify his approach, leading to a bewildering complexity of techniques and protocols which can be daunting to a beginner, and a challenge to the most experienced therapist. Erickson was adamant that we should not try to copy him, and said that we couldn't, in any case, even if we tried. Rather, he invited students to be more like themselves, to find their own way, their own style, their own voice.
In this 3 day intensive, CET with the Milton H Erickson Institute of Tasmania offers a unique opportunity to be immersed in the principles and applications of one of the most rapidly growing treatment models in contemporary psychotherapy. The scope of the program is comprehensive, and presents the essential elements in an elegantly accessible way. 
The 3 day intensive is open to practitioners of all levels of experience who are passionate about their work.
In this programme we will 
explore beautifully simple methods of inviting anyone into the experience of hypnosis and to connect with their unique resources in a dependably reliable way, 
generate ways of softening rigidities that keep problems stuck, and unique flexibilities in experience that hypnosis can contribute, 
in a mood of respectful lightness and safety so that whatever our levels of experience, we can find our own individual version of this method and apply it to our work readily and immediately.
I had the rare privilege of learning with Erickson and experienced first hand some of his vast complexity. I have made my own translation, simplifying some of the complexity to create a solid foundation and have the continuing joy of being part of a growing number of people who are developing their own complexities and style.
simple
hypnosis has been understood as complex, special, even dangerous, requiring unique restrictions. In this programme we will explore simple, elegant and respectful ways of connecting clients with their own unique styles of experience.
learning
There will be a minimum of theory and explanations, focusing instead on demonstrations and recurrent practice so that participants will have sufficient skills to begin to use their learning in their clinical practice immediately.
a natural approach
Erickson spoke of the everyday trance as a natural experience that can be enhanced in a clinical situation. By utilising individual clients’ individual skills, a fail-safe method will be made obvious and readily useable.
solutions
Ericksonian hypnosis creates a wonderful context for a trusting therapeutic relationship, helping to restore self-trust in individual clients by reconnecting them with their natural resourcefulness.
hypnosis
When we begin with the principle that hypnosis is an experience that involves focus of attention and absorption in that experience, this natural ability can be readily accessed by clients and incorporated in any clinician’s practice.
3 day intensive
In this 3 day workshop, we will enjoy sharing learning:
creating a mood of connection to enhance rapport,
exploring natural links between everyday happenings and the process of inviting a client into the experience of hypnosis,
establishing easy and natural relationships between specific client resources and their solutions.

### SiH diploma | Locations

Locations

		

	

	
	

		
	

	
	

		

			
		
 
		
		

			

			
Rob McNeilly MBBS 
was in a suburban Melbourne general medical practice for 10 years, had the privilege of learning directly with 
Milton Erickson
, was inspired by his human approach to therapy, and created his own interpretation to assist clients in a respectful, dignified way with the human dilemmas that affect individuals, couples and families. 
He founded the CET in l988 to introduce Ericksonian Hypnosis and the Solution Oriented Approach to hypnosis, counselling and coaching in Australia. He is co-author, with Jenny Brown, of "Healing With Words", "Healing the Whole Person" was published by Wiley, republished as “Doing Change” by St Luke’s Innovative Resources, and Crown House have published a series of DVD demonstrations of counselling and hypnosis.
